Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on modules structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 41. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 854 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to modules theory, we decompose the problem into 41 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 854. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the modules object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 722. The moduli space has dimension at most 662 with 1 connected components.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the modules space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 722 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 662. QED.
